                              ? 'bg-amber-400 shadow-md scale-110 border-2 border-amber-600' 
                              : 'hover:bg-amber-100'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {av}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 text-[11px] text-amber-900 font-medium">
                    💡 Sau khi đăng ký, hệ thống sẽ tự động cấp một <strong>Mã ID tài khoản của con</strong> (ví dụ: HS123). Ba mẹ chỉ cần dùng Tên bé và Mã ID này để đăng nhập vào xem báo cáo!
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : selectedRole === 'teacher' ? (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Email giáo viên:
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      placeholder="giaovien@truonghoc.edu.vn"
                      value={regEmail}
                      onChange={(e) => setRegEmail(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold focus:border-teal-500 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Mật khẩu khởi tạo:
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      required
                      placeholder="Tối thiểu 4 ký tự..."
                      value={regPassword}
                      onChange={(e) => setRegPassword(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold focus:border-teal-500 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Trường tiểu học công tác:
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      placeholder="Ví dụ: Trường Tiểu học Chu Văn An"
                      value={regSchool}
                      onChange={(e) => setRegSchool(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold focus:border-teal-500 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="p-4 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 rounded-2xl text-xs text-indigo-900">
                  Phụ huynh không cần đăng ký tài khoản riêng! Ba mẹ chỉ cần chuyển sang tab <strong>Đăng Nhập</strong> và nhập <strong>Tên của con + ID tài khoản của con</strong> là có thể vào xem báo cáo ngay.
                </div>
              )}

              {selectedRole !== 'parent' && (
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-600 hover:from-emerald-600 hover:to-teal-700 text-white font-black text-sm rounded-2xl shadow-md transition transform active:scale-98 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2"
                >
                  <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Hoàn Tất Đăng Ký</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </form>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer info */}
        <div className="bg-slate-50 px-5 py-3 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
          <div>
            Đang đăng nhập: <strong className="text-slate-800">{currentUser?.name || 'Khách'}</strong> ({currentUser?.role || 'Chưa đăng nhập'})
          </div>
          {currentUser && (
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                logout();
                onClose();
              }}
              className="text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700 font-bold underline text-xs"
            >
              Đăng xuất
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 4/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/AudioPlayer.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Volume2, VolumeX, Play, Pause, RotateCcw, FastForward } from 'lucide-react';
import { speakText, stopSpeech } from '../../utils/speech';

export const AudioPlayer = ({ 
  audioUrl, 
  speechText, 
  isEnglish = true, 
  title = "Nghe âm thanh", 
  onFinished,
  compact = false 
}) => {
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState(0.85); // primary school friendly default
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const audioRef = useRef(null);
  const progressIntervalRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      stopSpeech();
      if (progressIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(progressIntervalRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const handlePlayToggle = () => {
    if (isPlaying) {
      if (audioUrl && audioRef.current) {
        audioRef.current.pause();
      } else {
        stopSpeech();
      }
      setIsPlaying(false);
      if (progressIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(progressIntervalRef.current);
    } else {
      setIsPlaying(true);
      setProgress(0);

      if (audioUrl) {
        // Native audio element
        if (audioRef.current) {
          audioRef.current.playbackRate = speed;
          audioRef.current.play();
        }
      } else if (speechText) {
        // Web Speech API synthesis
        speakText(speechText, {
          lang: isEnglish ? 'en-US' : 'vi-VN',
          rate: speed,
          onEnd: () => {
            setIsPlaying(false);
            setProgress(100);
            if (progressIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(progressIntervalRef.current);
            if (onFinished) onFinished();
          }
        });

        // Simulate progress bar based on estimated length
        const estDuration = Math.max(1500, (speechText.length / 10) * 1000 / speed);
        const startTime = Date.now();
        progressIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
          const elapsed = Date.now() - startTime;
          const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((elapsed / estDuration) * 100));
          setProgress(pct);
          if (pct >= 100) clearInterval(progressIntervalRef.current);
        }, 100);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleReplay = () => {
    stopSpeech();
    if (audioRef.current) {
      audioRef.current.currentTime = 0;
    }
    setIsPlaying(false);
    setProgress(0);
    setTimeout(() => {
      handlePlayToggle();
    }, 150);
  };

  const changeSpeed = (newSpeed) => {
    setSpeed(newSpeed);
    if (audioRef.current) {
      audioRef.current.playbackRate = newSpeed;
    }
    if (isPlaying) {
      // restart with new speed
      handleReplay();
    }
  };

  if (compact) {
    return (
      <button 
        onClick={handlePlayToggle}
        title="Nghe phát âm"
        className={`p-2.5 rounded-full transition-all flex items-center justify-center ${
          isPlaying 
            ? 'bg-amber-400 text-amber-900 shadow-md animate-pulse ring-4 ring-amber-200' 
            : 'bg-sky-500 hover:bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-sm'
        }`}
      >
        {isPlaying ? <Pause className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Volume2 className="w-5 h-5" />}
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-50 to-indigo-50 border-2 border-sky-200 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <span className="p-2 bg-sky-500 text-white rounded-xl shadow-sm">
            <Volume2 className="w-5 h-5" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">{title}</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {isEnglish ? 'Web Speech API (Giọng bản xứ US)' : 'Giọng đọc Tiếng Việt'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Speed toggle */}
        <div className="flex items-center bg-white border border-sky-200 rounded-xl p-1 text-xs font-bold">
          <button 
            onClick={() => changeSpeed(0.7)}
            className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg ${speed === 0.7 ? 'bg-sky-500 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}`}
          >
            0.7x Chậm
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => changeSpeed(0.9)}
            className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg ${speed === 0.9 ? 'bg-sky-500 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}`}
          >
            Chuẩn
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => changeSpeed(1.1)}
            className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg ${speed === 1.1 ? 'bg-sky-500 text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'}`}
          >
            1.1x Nhanh
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Progress Bar */}
      <div className="w-full bg-sky-100 rounded-full h-2.5 my-3 overflow-hidden">
        <div 
          className="bg-sky-500 h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-150" 
          style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
        ></div>
      </div>

      {/* Controls */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-4">
        <button 
          onClick={handleReplay}
          className="p-2.5 text-slate-600 hover:text-sky-600 hover:bg-white rounded-xl transition"
          title="Nghe lại từ đầu"
        >
          <RotateCcw className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        <button 
          onClick={handlePlayToggle}
          className={`px-6 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-bold flex items-center space-x-2 transition shadow-md btn-kid ${
            isPlaying 
              ? 'bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500 text-amber-950' 
              : 'bg-sky-500 hover:bg-sky-600 text-white'
          }`}
        >
          {isPlaying ? (
            <>
              <Pause className="w-5 h-5" />
              <span>Tạm dừng</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Play className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" />
              <span>Nghe bài đọc</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {audioUrl && (
        <audio 
          ref={audioRef} 
          src={audioUrl} 
          onEnded={() => {
            setIsPlaying(false);
            setProgress(100);
            if (onFinished) onFinished();
          }}
          onTimeUpdate={() => {
            if (audioRef.current) {
              const pct = (audioRef.current.currentTime / audioRef.current.duration) * 100;
              setProgress(pct || 0);
            }
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 5/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/ErrorBoundary.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, Home } from 'lucide-react';

export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
  constructor(props) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error, errorInfo) {
    console.error('Lỗi giao diện (ErrorBoundary caught):', error, errorInfo);
    this.setState({ errorInfo });
  }

  handleReload = () => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  handleResetState = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_users');
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_students');
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_lessons');
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_exams');
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_classes');
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_current_user');
    localStorage.removeItem('vuihoc_role');
    window.location.reload();
  };

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 md:p-8 max-w-lg w-full shadow-2xl border-4 border-amber-300 text-center space-y-4">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-amber-100 text-amber-600 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-3xl">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-8 h-8" />
            </div>
            
            <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 font-display">
              Đang khôi phục trang học tập
            </h2>
            
            <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-slate-600 font-medium">
              Hệ thống phát hiện một trục trặc nhỏ và đã ngăn màn hình trắng. Bạn có thể bấm nút bên dưới để tải lại ngay.
            </p>

            {this.state.error && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 text-xs font-mono rounded-xl text-left overflow-x-auto max-h-32 border border-rose-200">
                {this.state.error.toString()}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                onClick={this.handleReload}
                className="flex-1 py-3 px-4 bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500 text-amber-950 font-black rounded-2xl shadow-md flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Tải Lại Trang</span>
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={this.handleResetState}
                className="py-3 px-4 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-2xl text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition"
              >
                <span>Khôi Phục Trạng Thái Ban Đầu</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 6/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/Header.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../../context/AppContext';
import { GRADE_LEVELS } from '../../data/mockData';
import { 
  Sparkles, 
  Flame, 
  Star, 
  BookOpen, 
  Headphones, 
  FileText, 
  Bookmark, 
  Gamepad2, 
  LineChart, 
  GraduationCap,
  ShieldCheck,
  LogOut,
  LogIn,
  UserPlus,
  Edit3
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AuthModal } from '../auth/AuthModal';
import { ProfileEditModal } from './ProfileEditModal';

export const Header = ({ currentTab, setCurrentTab }) => {
  const { 
    currentRole, 
    setCurrentRole, 
    currentUser,
    currentStudent, 
    selectedGrade, 
    setSelectedGrade,
    logout
  } = useApp();

  const [authConfig, setAuthConfig] = useState({ isOpen: false, mode: 'login', role: null });
  const [isProfileEditOpen, setIsProfileEditOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isLargeFont, setIsLargeFont] = useState(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem('vuihoc_large_font') === 'true';
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLargeFont) {
      document.body.classList.add('font-large-mode');
    } else {
      document.body.classList.remove('font-large-mode');
    }
    localStorage.setItem('vuihoc_large_font', isLargeFont);
  }, [isLargeFont]);

  const openLoginModal = (role = null) => {
    setAuthConfig({ isOpen: true, mode: 'login', role });
  };

  const openRegisterModal = (role = null) => {
    setAuthConfig({ isOpen: true, mode: 'register', role });
  };

  const isTeacher = currentRole === 'teacher' || currentUser?.role === 'teacher';
  const isParent = currentRole === 'parent' || currentUser?.role === 'parent';

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b-2 border-amber-200 shadow-sm">
      {/* Top Bar: Brand, Grade Pills, Text Size, Login/Register */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        {/* Brand Logo */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => setCurrentTab('home')}>
          <div className="w-11 h-11 bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 to-orange-400 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-md transform hover:rotate-6 transition">
            🎒
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="font-display font-black text-xl md:text-2xl text-slate-800 tracking-tight leading-none">
              Học Vui Cấp 1
            </h1>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-semibold hidden sm:block">
              Nền tảng học tập trực quan • Lớp 1 - 5
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Grade Selector Pills */}
        <div className="flex items-center bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-1 gap-1">
          {GRADE_LEVELS.map(g => (
            <button
              key={g.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedGrade(g.id)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center space-x-1 ${
                selectedGrade === g.id
                  ? 'bg-amber-400 text-amber-950 shadow-sm scale-105'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-amber-200/50'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{g.icon}</span>
              <span>Lớp {g.id}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Right Section: Student stats, Font Size Button, Login / Register Buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          {/* Student Stats (Streaks & Stars) */}
          {currentRole === 'student' && currentStudent && (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 hidden md:flex">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 bg-orange-100 border border-orange-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-2xl text-orange-700 font-extrabold text-xs shadow-xs">
                <Flame className="w-4 h-4 fill-orange-500 text-orange-500 animate-bounce-soft" />
                <span>{currentStudent.streak} ngày</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-2xl text-amber-800 font-extrabold text-xs shadow-xs">
                <Star className="w-4 h-4 fill-amber-400 text-amber-500" />
                <span>{currentStudent.stars}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Nút bật/tắt Chữ To & Nét cho các cháu */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsLargeFont(!isLargeFont)}
            className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-2xl border-2 text-xs font-black transition flex items-center space-x-1 ${
              isLargeFont
                ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-700 shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 border-slate-300 shadow-xs'
            }`}
            title="Bật/Tắt chế độ chữ to nét dành riêng cho các cháu tiểu học"
          >
            <span className="text-sm font-black">A+</span>
            <span className="hidden lg:inline">{isLargeFont ? 'Chữ Siêu Nét' : 'Chữ To Rõ'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* KHU VỰC NÚT ĐĂNG NHẬP & ĐĂNG KÝ RÕ RÀNG */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
            {currentUser ? (
              /* Khi đã đăng nhập */
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-white border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-2.5 py-1 shadow-xs">
                <span className="text-xl">{currentUser.avatar || '👤'}</span>
                <div className="text-left hidden sm:block">
                  <div className="font-black text-xs text-slate-900 leading-tight max-w-[120px] truncate">
                    {currentUser.name}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-teal-700">
                    {currentUser.role === 'teacher' ? '👩‍🏫 Giáo Viên' : currentUser.role === 'parent' ? '👨‍👩‍👧 Phụ Huynh' : '🐱 Học Sinh'}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="h-4 w-px bg-slate-200 mx-1"></div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => openLoginModal()}
                  className="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-amber-100 text-slate-700 hover:text-amber-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl transition"
                  title="Đổi tài khoản"
                >
                  Đổi
                </button>

                <button
                  onClick={logout}
                  className="p-1 hover:bg-rose-100 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 rounded-xl transition"
                  title="Đăng xuất"
                >
                  <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              /* Khi chưa đăng nhập: Nút Đăng Nhập & Đăng Ký nổi bật */
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <button
                  onClick={() => openLoginModal()}
                  className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-orange-400 hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-500 text-amber-950 font-black text-xs rounded-2xl shadow-sm transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 transform hover:scale-102"
                >
                  <LogIn className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-950" />
                  <span>Đăng Nhập</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  onClick={() => openRegisterModal()}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-400 text-amber-900 font-black text-xs rounded-2xl shadow-xs transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 transform hover:scale-102"
                >
                  <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                  <span>Đăng Ký</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Navigation Tabs */}
      <nav className="bg-amber-100/60 border-t border-amber-200 px-4 py-1.5 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-none">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center space-x-1 sm:space-x-2 min-w-max">
          {/* CÁC TAB HỌC TẬP CHO HỌC SINH */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setCurrentTab('home')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
              currentTab === 'home' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-amber-950 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white/80'
            }`}
          >
            <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Môn Học Cấp 1</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setCurrentTab('english')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
              currentTab === 'english' ? 'bg-sky-500 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white/80'
            }`}
          >
            <Headphones className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Khu Luyện Nghe Tiếng Anh</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setCurrentTab('exams')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
              currentTab === 'exams' ? 'bg-rose-500 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white/80'
            }`}
          >
            <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Làm Đề Thi & Đánh Giá</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setCurrentTab('errors')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
              currentTab === 'errors' ? 'bg-purple-500 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white/80'
            }`}
          >
            <Bookmark className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Sổ Lỗi Sai</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setCurrentTab('games')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
              currentTab === 'games' ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-white/80'
            }`}
          >
            <Gamepad2 className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Góc Trò Chơi</span>
          </button>

          {/* TAB DÀNH CHO PHỤ HUYNH (CHỈ HIỂN THỊ KHI LÀ PHỤ HUYNH) */}
          {isParent && (
            <>
              <div className="h-5 w-px bg-amber-300 mx-1"></div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setCurrentTab('parent')}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-extrabold transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
                  currentTab === 'parent' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-indigo-900 bg-indigo-100 hover:bg-indigo-200'
                }`}
              >
                <LineChart className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Báo Cáo Phụ Huynh</span>
              </button>
            </>
          )}

          {/* TAB QUẢN LÝ WEB DÀNH RIÊNG CHO GIÁO VIÊN (CHỈ HIỂN THỊ KHI LÀ GIÁO VIÊN) */}
          {isTeacher && (
            <>
              <div className="h-5 w-px bg-amber-300 mx-1"></div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setCurrentTab('admin')}
                className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-black transition flex items-center space-x-1.5 ${
                  currentTab === 'admin' 
                    ? 'bg-teal-700 text-white shadow-md ring-2 ring-teal-400' 
                    : 'text-teal-950 bg-teal-100 hover:bg-teal-200 border border-teal-300'
                }`}
              >
                <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-700 group-hover:text-white" />
                <span>Quản Lý Web (Giáo Viên)</span>
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </nav>

      {/* Modal Đăng Nhập & Đăng Ký */}
      <AuthModal 
        isOpen={authConfig.isOpen} 
        onClose={() => setAuthConfig({ ...authConfig, isOpen: false })} 
        initialMode={authConfig.mode}
        initialRole={authConfig.role}
      />
    </header>
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 7/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/LoginModal.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../../context/AppContext';
import { User, Lock, X, Check, Sparkles, School } from 'lucide-react';

export const LoginModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { students, switchStudent, setCurrentRole, currentRole } = useApp();
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState('student');
  const [selectedStudentId, setSelectedStudentId] = useState(students[0]?.id || 's1');
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleStudentLogin = (student) => {
    // Direct switch or check PIN
    if (student.pin && pin && pin !== student.pin) {
      setErrorMsg('Mã PIN không đúng bé ơi!');
      return;
    }
    switchStudent(student.id);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleParentTeacherLogin = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) {
      setErrorMsg('Vui lòng nhập đầy đủ thông tin đăng nhập.');
      return;
    }
    // Mock authentication
    setCurrentRole(selectedRole);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-4xl max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl border-4 border-amber-300 relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Close button */}
        <button 
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 rounded-full hover:bg-slate-100 transition"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <div className="inline-block p-3 bg-amber-100 rounded-3xl text-3xl mb-2">
            🎒
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-800 font-display">Chọn Vai Trò Đăng Nhập</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Dễ dàng, bảo mật và an toàn cho bé</p>
        </div>

        {/* Role Tabs */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 p-1.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-2xl mb-6">
          <button
            onClick={() => { setSelectedRole('student'); setErrorMsg(''); }}
            className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs md:text-sm transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 ${
              selectedRole === 'student' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-amber-950 shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <span>👦</span>
            <span>Học sinh</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setSelectedRole('parent'); setErrorMsg(''); }}
            className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs md:text-sm transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 ${
              selectedRole === 'parent' ? 'bg-sky-500 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <span>👨‍👩‍👧</span>
            <span>Phụ huynh</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setSelectedRole('teacher'); setErrorMsg(''); }}
            className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs md:text-sm transition flex flex-col items-center gap-1 ${
              selectedRole === 'teacher' ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <span>👩‍🏫</span>
            <span>Giáo viên</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-600 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl text-center">
            {errorMsg}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Role Content */}
        {selectedRole === 'student' ? (
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-3 text-center">
              Chọn tài khoản hoặc nhập mã PIN 4 số để vào học:
            </p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">
              {students.map(st => (
                <div
                  key={st.id}
                  onClick={() => handleStudentLogin(st)}
                  className="cursor-pointer bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 border-2 border-amber-200 hover:border-amber-400 rounded-2xl p-3 text-center transition group cartoon-card"
                >
                  <div className="text-4xl mb-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                    {st.avatar}
                  </div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-800">Khối Lớp {st.grade}</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-amber-700 font-bold bg-amber-200/70 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 mt-1 inline-block">
                    {st.stars} ⭐
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Mã PIN bảo mật (tùy chọn):</label>
              <input
                type="password"
                maxLength={4}
                value={pin}
                onChange={(e) => setPin(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ví dụ: 1234"
                className="w-full text-center tracking-widest font-black text-xl px-4 py-2 bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl focus:border-amber-400 focus:outline-none"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleParentTeacherLogin} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Email đăng nhập:</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={selectedRole === 'parent' ? 'phuhuynh@gmail.com' : 'giaovien@school.edu.vn'}
                className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-sm bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl focus:border-sky-400 focus:outline-none font-medium"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 mb-1">Mật khẩu:</label>
              <input
                type="password"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-sm bg-slate-50 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-2xl focus:border-sky-400 focus:outline-none font-medium"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-blue-50 rounded-xl text-[11px] text-blue-700 font-medium">
              💡 Demo: Bạn có thể nhập email/mật khẩu bất kỳ để trải nghiệm ngay quyền Phụ huynh hoặc Giáo viên.
            </div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-orange-400 hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-500 text-amber-950 font-black rounded-2xl shadow-md btn-kid text-base"
            >
              Đăng nhập ngay
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 8/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/MultiItemQuestion.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, Sparkles, Check, AlertCircle, Maximize2, X } from 'lucide-react';

export const MultiItemQuestion = ({
  question,
  userAnswers = {},
  onAnswerChange,
  isSubmitted = false,
  disabled = false
}) => {
  const [isZoomOpen, setIsZoomOpen] = useState(false);
  const subQuestions = question.subQuestions || [];
  if (subQuestions.length === 0) return null;

  // Tính kết quả sau khi nộp bài
  let correctCount = 0;
  let earnedScore = 0;
  let totalScore = 0;

  subQuestions.forEach((sub, idx) => {
    const subKey = `${question.id}_${sub.id || idx}`;
    const userVal = String(userAnswers[subKey] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const expectedVal = String(sub.answer || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const p = typeof sub.points === 'number' ? sub.points : (question.points || 2) / subQuestions.length;
    totalScore += p;

    if (userVal && expectedVal && (userVal === expectedVal || userVal.replace(/\s+/g, '') === expectedVal.replace(/\s+/g, ''))) {
      correctCount += 1;
      earnedScore += p;
    }
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Banner kết quả chi tiết sau khi nộp bài */}
      {isSubmitted && (
        <div className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between border-2 animate-fade-in ${
          correctCount === subQuestions.length
            ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300 text-emerald-900'
            : correctCount > 0
            ? 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-900'
            : 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-300 text-rose-900'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            {correctCount === subQuestions.length ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
            ) : correctCount > 0 ? (
              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 shrink-0" />
            )}
            <span className="font-black text-xs md:text-sm">
              Kết quả: Đúng {correctCount}/{subQuestions.length} ý
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="font-extrabold text-xs px-2.5 py-1 bg-white/80 rounded-xl shadow-xs">
            +{Math.round(earnedScore * 100) / 100} / {Math.round(totalScore * 100) / 100} điểm
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Hình ảnh minh họa cho câu hỏi nhiều ý */}
      {question.image && (
        <div className="my-2 flex flex-col items-center">
          <div 
            onClick={() => setIsZoomOpen(true)}
            className="relative cursor-pointer group rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/50 p-1 shadow-xs hover:border-amber-400 transition"
            title="Bấm để xem ảnh phóng to sắc nét"
          >
            <img 
              src={question.image} 
              alt="Hình ảnh minh họa" 
              className={`rounded-xl object-contain mx-auto ${
                question.imageSize === 'small' ? 'max-h-36 max-w-xs' : question.imageSize === 'large' ? 'max-h-96 w-full' : 'max-h-60 max-w-md'
              }`}
            />
            <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-slate-900/70 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center space-x-1 backdrop-blur-xs">
              <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>Phóng to</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal Lightbox phóng to ảnh cho các cháu xem rõ */}
      {isZoomOpen && (
        <div 
          onClick={() => setIsZoomOpen(false)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-fade-in"
        >
          <div className="relative max-w-4xl max-h-[90vh] bg-white rounded-3xl p-3 shadow-2xl border-4 border-amber-400 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsZoomOpen(false)}
              className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-10 w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-slate-900/80 hover:bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center transition"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
            <div className="overflow-auto flex items-center justify-center p-2">
              <img 
                src={question.image} 
                alt="Hình minh họa phóng to" 
                className="max-h-[80vh] w-auto object-contain rounded-2xl"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold py-1">
              Hình ảnh minh họa sắc nét • Bấm bất kỳ đâu để đóng lại
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid danh sách các ý nhỏ (1 cột trên mobile, 2 cột trên máy tính) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:gap-4">
        {subQuestions.map((sub, idx) => {
          const subKey = `${question.id}_${sub.id || idx}`;
          const currentVal = userAnswers[subKey] || '';
          const userVal = String(currentVal).trim().toLowerCase();
          const expectedVal = String(sub.answer || '').trim().toLowerCase();
          const isItemCorrect = isSubmitted && userVal && expectedVal && (
            userVal === expectedVal || userVal.replace(/\s+/g, '') === expectedVal.replace(/\s+/g, '')
          );
          const isItemWrong = isSubmitted && !isItemCorrect;

          return (
            <div
              key={sub.id || idx}
              className={`p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between ${
                isSubmitted
                  ? isItemCorrect
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50/60 border-emerald-400 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-emerald-300'
                    : 'bg-rose-50/60 border-rose-400 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-rose-300'
                  : currentVal
                  ? 'bg-amber-50/50 border-amber-400 shadow-xs'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-amber-300 shadow-xs'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
                {/* Nhãn thứ tự ý */}
                <span className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">
                  {sub.label ? `Ý ${sub.label}` : `Ý ${idx + 1}`}
                </span>

                {/* Điểm số của ý này */}
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400">
                  ({sub.points || Math.round(((question.points || 2) / subQuestions.length) * 100) / 100} đ)
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Phép tính / Lời dẫn của ý & Ô nhập đáp án */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 my-1">
                <span className="font-black text-slate-800 text-base md:text-lg tracking-wide shrink-0 font-display">
                  {sub.prompt}
                </span>

                <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-[150px]">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    disabled={disabled || isSubmitted}
                    tabIndex={idx + 1}
                    value={currentVal}
                    onChange={(e) => onAnswerChange(subKey, e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Đáp án..."
                    className={`w-full h-11 px-3 text-center text-base md:text-lg font-black rounded-xl border-2 transition focus:outline-none ${
                      isSubmitted
                        ? isItemCorrect
                          ? 'bg-emerald-100 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-950'
                          : 'bg-rose-100 border-rose-500 text-rose-950 line-through'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-300 text-slate-900 focus:border-amber-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-200'
                    }`}
                  />
                  {isSubmitted && isItemCorrect && (
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 absolute right-2 top-3.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  )}
                  {isSubmitted && isItemWrong && (
                    <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 absolute right-2 top-3.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Lời giải hoặc đáp án đúng hiển thị sau khi nộp bài */}
              {isSubmitted && isItemWrong && (
                <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-rose-200 text-xs font-bold text-rose-700 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span>Đáp án đúng:</span>
                  <span className="bg-rose-200/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-rose-950 font-black">
                    {sub.answer || 'Chưa cài đặt'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 9/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/PetCompanion.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';

    quotes: ['Ăn xong lá trúc rồi, cùng luyện tập thôi!', 'Bình tĩnh suy nghĩ là ra đáp án ngay!', 'Bé giỏi quá đi mất thôi!'],

export const PetCompanion = ({ pet }) => {
  const { currentStudent, activeStudentId, updatePet } = useApp();

  const [showQuote, setShowQuote] = useState(false);
  const [quoteIndex, setQuoteIndex] = useState(0);
  const [isChangeModalOpen, setIsChangeModalOpen] = useState(false);

  // Active pet data
  const currentPetData = pet || currentStudent?.pet || { type: 'cat', name: 'Miu Miu', level: 1, exp: 0, maxExp: 80 };
  const petInfo = PET_CATALOG[currentPetData.type] || PET_CATALOG.cat;
  const stageName = petInfo.stages[Math.min(currentPetData.level - 1, petInfo.stages.length - 1)] || 'Bạn Đồng Hành';

  // Form states cho Modal Đổi Thú Cưng
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState(currentPetData.type || 'cat');
  const [petCustomName, setPetCustomName] = useState(currentPetData.name || petInfo.defaultName);

  const handlePetClick = () => {
    setShowQuote(true);
    setQuoteIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % petInfo.quotes.length);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setShowQuote(false);
    }, 4000);
  };

  const handleOpenModal = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setSelectedType(currentPetData.type || 'cat');
    setPetCustomName(currentPetData.name || petInfo.defaultName);
    setIsChangeModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSelectAnimal = (typeKey) => {
    setSelectedType(typeKey);
    // Nếu chưa đổi tên riêng thì gợi ý tên mặc định của loài đó
    const newAnimal = PET_CATALOG[typeKey];
    if (newAnimal) {
      setPetCustomName(newAnimal.defaultName);
    }
  };

  const handleSavePet = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const finalName = petCustomName.trim() || PET_CATALOG[selectedType]?.defaultName || 'Bạn Thú';

    if (updatePet) {
      const studentIdToUpdate = currentStudent?.id || activeStudentId || 's1';
      updatePet(studentIdToUpdate, {
        type: selectedType,
        name: finalName
      });
    }

    confetti({ particleCount: 70, spread: 60 });
    setIsChangeModalOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div 
        onClick={handleOpenModal}
        className="relative bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-50 to-orange-50 border-3 border-amber-300 hover:border-amber-400 rounded-3xl p-4 shadow-sm cartoon-card cursor-pointer transition group"
        title="Bấm vào để đổi loài thú cưng hoặc đặt tên cho bé thú!"
      >
        {/* Nút Đổi Thú Cưng ở góc trên thẻ */}
        <button
          onClick={handleOpenModal}
          type="button"
          className="absolute -top-2.5 -right-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-orange-400 hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-500 text-amber-950 font-black text-[11px] px-3 py-1 rounded-full border-2 border-white shadow-md flex items-center space-x-1.5 transition transform group-hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 z-10"
          title="Bấm để đổi bạn thú cưng và đổi tên bạn thú!"
        >
          <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin-slow text-amber-950" />
          <span>Đổi Thú & Tên 🐾</span>
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          {/* Pet Avatar with interactive bounce & click to talk */}
          <div 
            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handlePetClick(); }}
            className="relative cursor-pointer group/avatar shrink-0"
            title="Bấm vào để trò chuyện cùng bạn thú cưng!"
          >
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-white rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-4xl shadow-inner border-2 border-amber-200 group-hover/avatar:scale-110 transition-transform">
              {petInfo.avatar}
            </div>
            <span className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 bg-amber-500 text-white text-[10px] font-black px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-white shadow-xs">
              Lv.{currentPetData.level || 1}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Pet Info & Exp */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-amber-700 uppercase tracking-wider block truncate">
                  {stageName}
                </span>
                <h4 
                  className="font-black text-slate-900 text-base flex items-center gap-1 truncate group-hover:text-amber-700 transition"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{currentPetData.name}</span>
                  <Heart className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-500 fill-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                </h4>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-700 bg-amber-100 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg shrink-0">
                {currentPetData.exp || 0}/{currentPetData.maxExp || 80} EXP
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Exp Bar */}
            <div className="w-full bg-amber-100 rounded-full h-2.5 mt-1.5 overflow-hidden border border-amber-200">
              <div 
                className="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-orange-500 h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
                style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, Math.round(((currentPetData.exp || 0) / (currentPetData.maxExp || 80)) * 100))}%` }}
              ></div>
            </div>

            <div className="text-[10px] text-amber-800/80 font-bold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
              <span>✏️ Chạm để đổi tên & chọn thú cưng</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Speech Bubble when pet is clicked */}
        {showQuote && (
          <div className="absolute -top-14 left-4 right-4 bg-white border-2 border-amber-400 rounded-2xl px-3 py-2 shadow-xl text-xs font-black text-amber-950 animate-bounce z-20 text-center">
            {petInfo.quotes[quoteIndex]}
            <div className="absolute -bottom-2 left-8 w-3 h-3 bg-white border-b-2 border-r-2 border-amber-400 transform rotate-45"></div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MODAL: CHỌN & ĐỔI BẠN THÚ CƯNG (CĂN GIỮA MÀN HÌNH) */}
      {isChangeModalOpen && createPortal(
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] overflow-y-auto bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-6">
          <div 
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} 
            className="relative my-auto bg-white w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border-4 border-amber-400 overflow-hidden text-slate-800 animate-scale-up"
          >
            {/* Header Modal */}
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-orange-400 to-amber-500 p-4 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <span className="text-2xl">🐾</span>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-display font-black text-base md:text-lg leading-tight">
                    Chọn & Đổi Bạn Thú Cưng Của Bé
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-100 font-medium">
                    Chọn người bạn đồng hành và đặt tên theo sở thích của bé!
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button 
                onClick={() => setIsChangeModalOpen(false)}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/40 flex items-center justify-center text-white transition"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Form chọn thú cưng */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSavePet} className="p-5 space-y-4">
              {/* Danh sách 9 bạn thú cưng */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600 mb-2">
                  1. Chọn bạn thú cưng yêu thích của bé:
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
                  {Object.values(PET_CATALOG).map(animal => {
                    const isSelected = selectedType === animal.id;
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={animal.id}
                        onClick={() => handleSelectAnimal(animal.id)}
                        className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-between ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50 shadow-md ring-2 ring-amber-300 scale-102'
                            : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-amber-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="text-3xl mb-1 transform group-hover:scale-110 transition">
                          {animal.avatar}
                        </div>
                        <div className="font-black text-xs text-slate-800 leading-tight">
                          {animal.stages[0]}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-amber-700 font-bold mt-0.5">
                          Tên: {animal.defaultName}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Ô đặt tên cho bạn thú cưng */}
              <div className="bg-amber-50/70 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 space-y-2">
                <label className="block text-xs font-black text-amber-950">
                  2. Đặt tên riêng cho bạn thú cưng của bé:
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    placeholder="Ví dụ: Miu Miu, Cún Bắp, Bé Đậu Đậu..."
                    value={petCustomName}
                    onChange={(e) => setPetCustomName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-black focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none bg-white shadow-inner"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-amber-800 font-medium italic">
                  💡 Lời nhắn từ {petCustomName || 'bạn thú'}: "{PET_CATALOG[selectedType]?.quotes[0]}"
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Nút lưu */}
              <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end space-x-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsChangeModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition"
                >
                  Để Sau
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white font-black rounded-xl text-xs shadow-md transition transform active:scale-95 flex items-center space-x-1.5"
                >
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Chọn Bạn Này Làm Bạn Đồng Hành!</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>,
        document.body
      )}
    </>
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 10/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/ProfileEditModal.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { useApp } from '../../context/AppContext';
import { X, Edit3, Check, KeyRound, User, Lock, Smile, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

const AVATAR_LIST = ['🐱', '🐶', '🐰', '🦊', '🐼', '🦁', '🐯', '🦄', '🐨', '🐸', '🐧', '🦉', '👩‍🏫', '👨‍🏫', '👨‍👩‍👧'];

export const ProfileEditModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { currentUser, updateUser } = useApp();

  const [name, setName] = useState(currentUser?.name || '');
  const [username, setUsername] = useState(currentUser?.username || '');
  const [studentId, setStudentId] = useState(currentUser?.studentId || '');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState(currentUser?.password || currentUser?.pin || '123');
  const [grade, setGrade] = useState(currentUser?.grade || 1);
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(currentUser?.avatar || '🐱');
  const [school, setSchool] = useState(currentUser?.school || '');
  const [notifyMsg, setNotifyMsg] = useState('');

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (currentUser) {
      setName(currentUser.name || '');
      setUsername(currentUser.username || '');
      setStudentId(currentUser.studentId || '');
      setPassword(currentUser.password || currentUser.pin || '123');
      setGrade(currentUser.grade || 1);
      setAvatar(currentUser.avatar || '🐱');
      setSchool(currentUser.school || '');
      setNotifyMsg('');
    }
  }, [currentUser, isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen || !currentUser) return null;

  const handleSave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim()) return;

    updateUser(currentUser.id, {
      name: name.trim(),
      username: username ? username.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined,
      studentId: studentId ? studentId.trim().toUpperCase() : undefined,
      password: password.trim(),
      grade: Number(grade),
      avatar,
      school: school.trim()
    });

    setNotifyMsg('✨ Đã cập nhật và đổi thông tin thành công!');
    setTimeout(() => {
      onClose();
    }, 800);
  };

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm overflow-y-auto">
      <div 
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} 
        className="relative m-auto bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border-4 border-amber-400 overflow-hidden text-slate-800"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-orange-400 p-4 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="text-2xl">{avatar}</span>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-black text-sm md:text-base leading-tight">Đổi Thông Tin & Mật Khẩu</h3>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-100 font-medium">Cập nhật hồ sơ tài khoản của bạn</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/40 flex items-center justify-center text-white"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {notifyMsg && (
          <div className="m-4 p-3 bg-emerald-50 border-2 border-emerald-300 rounded-2xl text-emerald-800 text-xs font-bold flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
            <span>{notifyMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="p-5 space-y-3.5 text-xs">
          {/* Đổi Họ và tên */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Họ và Tên:</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          {currentUser.role === 'student' ? (
            <>
              {/* Đổi Tên tài khoản & Mã ID của con */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Tên tài khoản (đăng nhập):</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={username}
                    onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
                    placeholder="VD: hocsinh1"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Mã ID con (cho phụ huynh):</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={studentId}
                    onChange={(e) => setStudentId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none uppercase"
                    placeholder="VD: HS001"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Đổi Mật khẩu & Khối lớp */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Mật khẩu của bé:</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Khối Lớp:</label>
                  <select
                    value={grade}
                    onChange={(e) => setGrade(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
                  >
                    {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(g => (
                      <option key={g} value={g}>Lớp {g}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              {/* Đổi Mật khẩu giáo viên / phụ huynh */}
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Mật khẩu mới:</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              {currentUser.role === 'teacher' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Trường học công tác:</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={school}
                    onChange={(e) => setSchool(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold focus:border-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}

          {/* Chọn Đổi Avatar */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Đổi Avatar hình đại diện:</label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 p-2 bg-slate-50 border rounded-2xl">
              {AVATAR_LIST.map(av => (
                <button
                  key={av}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAvatar(av)}
                  className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl text-lg flex items-center justify-center transition ${
                    avatar === av 
                      ? 'bg-amber-400 border-2 border-amber-600 scale-110 shadow-sm' 
                      : 'hover:bg-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {av}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end space-x-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-bold rounded-xl"
            >
              Hủy
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white font-black rounded-xl shadow-md transition"
            >
              Lưu Thay Đổi
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 11/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/common/VoiceGuideButton.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Volume2, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { speakText } from '../../utils/speech';

export const VoiceGuideButton = ({ text, tooltip = "Bấm để nghe cô đọc hướng dẫn", className = "" }) => {
  const [speaking, setSpeaking] = useState(false);

  const handleClick = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!text) return;
    setSpeaking(true);
    speakText(text, {
      lang: 'vi-VN',
      rate: 0.85,
      pitch: 1.1,
      onEnd: () => setSpeaking(false)
    });
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleClick}
      title={tooltip}
      className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full font-bold text-xs bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 border-2 border-amber-300 transition-all shadow-sm ${className}`}
    >
      {speaking ? (
        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-amber-600" />
      ) : (
        <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 animate-pulse" />
      )}
      <span>Đọc hướng dẫn</span>
    </button>
  );
};


/* =============================================================================
 * [FILE 12/34] Đường dẫn: src/components/exam/ExamBankAndTaker.jsx
 * ============================================================================= */

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../../context/AppContext';
import { AudioPlayer } from '../common/AudioPlayer';
import { MultiItemQuestion } from '../common/MultiItemQuestion';
import { gradeMultiItemQuestion } from '../../utils/multiItemSplitter';
import { playKidSound } from '../../utils/speech';
import { 
  FileText, 
  Timer, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  XCircle, 
  RotateCcw, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  ArrowRight,
  HelpCircle, 
  Headphones, 
  Trophy,
  UploadCloud,
  Check,
  Maximize2,
  X,
  AlertTriangle,
  Layers,
  Sparkles
} from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';

export const ExamBankAndTaker = () => {
  const { 
    exams, 
    selectedGrade, 
    addToErrorNotebook, 
    rewardStars, 
    allSubjects, 
    recordExamResult,
    currentStudent 
  } = useApp();

  const [activeExam, setActiveExam] = useState(null);
  const [userAnswers, setUserAnswers] = useState({});
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(0);
  const [isExamFinished, setIsExamFinished] = useState(false);
  const [examResult, setExamResult] = useState(null);

  // Filters for exam bank
  const [filterSubject, setFilterSubject] = useState('all');
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'practice' | 'test' | 'review'

  // Question navigation modes: 'single' (từng câu) | 'all' (toàn bộ đề)
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState('single');
  const [currentQIndex, setCurrentQIndex] = useState(0);

  // Modal zoom ảnh lớn
  const [zoomImage, setZoomImage] = useState(null);

  // Modal xác nhận nộp bài khi còn câu chưa làm
  const [showSubmitConfirm, setShowSubmitConfirm] = useState(false);

  // Filter exams by grade, status ('published' only for student view), subject, type
  const availableExams = (exams || []).filter(e => {
    const matchGrade = Number(e.grade) === Number(selectedGrade) || !e.grade;
    const isPublished = e.status !== 'draft' && e.status !== 'hidden';
    const matchSubject = filterSubject === 'all' || e.subjectId === filterSubject;
    const matchType = filterType === 'all' || e.examType === filterType;
    return matchGrade && isPublished && matchSubject && matchType;
  });

  // Timer countdown
  useEffect(() => {
    let timer;
    if (activeExam && !isExamFinished && timeLeft > 0) {
      timer = setInterval(() => {
        setTimeLeft(prev => prev - 1);
      }, 1000);
    } else if (timeLeft === 0 && activeExam && !isExamFinished && activeExam.durationMinutes > 0) {
      handleSubmitExam();
    }
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [activeExam, isExamFinished, timeLeft]);

  const handleStartExam = (exam) => {
    setActiveExam(exam);
    setUserAnswers({});
    const initialSeconds = (exam.durationMinutes || 20) * 60;
    setTimeLeft(initialSeconds);
    setIsExamFinished(false);
    setExamResult(null);
    setCurrentQIndex(0);
    setViewMode('single');
    setShowSubmitConfirm(false);
  };

  const handleSelectAnswer = (qId, ans) => {
    if (isExamFinished) return;
    setUserAnswers(prev => ({ ...prev, [qId]: ans }));
  };

  // Kiểm tra số câu đã trả lời
  const getAnsweredCount = () => {
    if (!activeExam || !activeExam.questions) return { answered: 0, total: 0 };
    const total = activeExam.questions.length;
    let answered = 0;
    activeExam.questions.forEach(q => {
      if (q.subQuestions && q.subQuestions.length > 0) {
        const hasAnySub = q.subQuestions.some((s, idx) => !!userAnswers[`${q.id}_${s.id || idx}`]);
        if (hasAnySub) answered++;
      } else if (userAnswers[q.id] && String(userAnswers[q.id]).trim().length > 0) {
        answered++;
      }
    });
    return { answered, total };
  };

  const handleCheckBeforeSubmit = () => {
    const { answered, total } = getAnsweredCount();
    if (answered < total) {
      setShowSubmitConfirm(true);
    } else {
      handleSubmitExam();
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitExam = () => {
    if (!activeExam) return;
    setShowSubmitConfirm(false);

    let earnedPoints = 0;
    let totalPoints = 0;
    let correctCount = 0;
    let wrongCount = 0;

    activeExam.questions.forEach(q => {
      if (q.subQuestions && q.subQuestions.length > 0) {
        // Chấm điểm từng ý độc lập cho câu hỏi có nhiều ý nhỏ
        const gradeRes = gradeMultiItemQuestion(q, userAnswers);
        earnedPoints += gradeRes.earnedPoints;
        totalPoints += gradeRes.totalPoints;
        if (gradeRes.isAllCorrect) {
          correctCount++;
        } else {
          wrongCount++;
        }

        // Tự động lưu các ý sai vào Sổ Lỗi Sai
        gradeRes.details.forEach(d => {
          if (!d.isCorrect) {
            addToErrorNotebook({
              subject: activeExam.subjectId === 'english' ? 'Tiếng Anh' : activeExam.subjectId === 'vietnamese' ? 'Tiếng Việt' : 'Toán',
              grade: activeExam.grade,
              question: `${q.question} (${d.prompt})`,
              userWrongAnswer: d.userAns || '(Chưa điền)',
              correctAnswer: d.expectedAns || 'Chưa cài đặt',
              explanation: d.explanation || q.explanation || 'Bé hãy đọc kĩ lại bài học và luyện tập thêm nhé!'
            });
          }
        });
      } else {
        const p = q.points || 2;
        totalPoints += p;
        const userAns = userAnswers[q.id];
        let correct = false;

        if (q.type === 'multiple-choice' || q.type === 'listen-choice') {
          correct = userAns === q.answer;
        } else if (q.type === 'fill-in' || q.type === 'listen-dictation') {
          correct = userAns && userAns.trim().toLowerCase() === String(q.answer).trim().toLowerCase();
        } else if (q.type === 'essay') {
          correct = !!userAns && userAns.trim().length > 3;
        }

        if (correct) {
          earnedPoints += p;
          correctCount++;
        } else {
          wrongCount++;
          addToErrorNotebook({
            subject: activeExam.subjectId === 'english' ? 'Tiếng Anh' : activeExam.subjectId === 'vietnamese' ? 'Tiếng Việt' : 'Toán',
            grade: activeExam.grade,
            question: q.question || 'Câu hỏi đề thi',
            userWrongAnswer: userAns || '(Bỏ trống)',
            correctAnswer: q.answer || q.sampleAnswer || 'Đáp án mẫu',
            explanation: q.explanation || 'Xem lại hướng dẫn làm bài.'
          });
        }
      }
    });

    const finalScore = Math.round((earnedPoints / (totalPoints || 10)) * 10 * 10) / 10;
    const timeSpentSeconds = ((activeExam.durationMinutes || 20) * 60) - timeLeft;

    setExamResult({
      score: finalScore,
      earnedPoints: Math.round(earnedPoints * 100) / 100,
      totalPoints: Math.round(totalPoints * 100) / 100,
      correctCount,
      wrongCount,
      totalQuestions: activeExam.questions.length
    });
    setIsExamFinished(true);

    // Ghi nhận kết quả làm bài vào cơ sở dữ liệu để Phụ huynh theo dõi
    if (recordExamResult) {
      recordExamResult({
        examId: activeExam.id,
        examTitle: activeExam.title,
        examType: activeExam.examType || 'practice',
        subjectId: activeExam.subjectId,
        grade: activeExam.grade,
        score: finalScore,
        earnedPoints: Math.round(earnedPoints * 100) / 100,
        totalPoints: Math.round(totalPoints * 100) / 100,
        correctCount,
        wrongCount,
        timeTakenSeconds: Math.max(10, timeSpentSeconds)
      });
    }

    if (finalScore >= 7) {
      playKidSound('fanfare');
      confetti({ particleCount: 120, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 } });
      rewardStars(15, 'Hoàn thành xuất sắc bài thi!');
    } else {
      playKidSound('wrong');
    }
  };

  const formatTimer = (seconds) => {
    if (seconds <= 0) return '00:00';
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const secs = seconds % 60;
    return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  // Card hiển thị một câu hỏi
  const renderQuestionCard = (q, displayIndex) => {
    const userAns = userAnswers[q.id];
    const isMulti = q.subQuestions && q.subQuestions.length > 0;
    let isCorrect = false;
    let isPartial = false;

    if (isExamFinished) {
      if (isMulti) {
        const gradeRes = gradeMultiItemQuestion(q, userAnswers);
        isCorrect = gradeRes.isAllCorrect;
        isPartial = !isCorrect && gradeRes.correctCount > 0;
      } else if (q.type === 'essay') {
        isCorrect = !!userAns && userAns.trim().length > 3;
      } else {
        isCorrect = userAns && q.answer && userAns.trim().toLowerCase() === String(q.answer).trim().toLowerCase();
      }
    }

    return (
      <div
        key={q.id || displayIndex}
        className={`bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-6 border-3 transition cartoon-card relative ${
          isExamFinished
            ? isCorrect
              ? 'border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50/20'
              : isPartial
              ? 'border-amber-300 bg-amber-50/20'
              : 'border-rose-300 bg-rose-50/20'
            : 'border-slate-200 shadow-sm'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Tiêu đề & Thứ tự câu hỏi */}
        <div className="mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
            <span className="w-9 h-9 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-rose-500 to-pink-500 text-white font-black flex items-center justify-center text-sm shadow-sm shrink-0">
              {displayIndex + 1}
            </span>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <h4 className="font-black text-slate-900 text-lg md:text-xl leading-snug font-display">
                  {q.question}
                </h4>
                {isMulti && (
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-black px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 shrink-0">
                    ⚡ {q.subQuestions.length} ý nhỏ
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">
                ({q.points || 2} điểm)
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Hình ảnh minh họa câu hỏi sắc nét (có nút phóng to) */}
        {q.image && (
          <div className="my-3 flex flex-col items-center">
            <div 
              onClick={() => setZoomImage(q.image)}
              className="relative cursor-pointer group rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/40 p-1 shadow-xs hover:border-amber-400 transition"
              title="Bấm để xem ảnh phóng to sắc nét"
            >
              <img 
                src={q.image} 
                alt="Hình minh họa" 
                className={`rounded-xl object-contain mx-auto ${
                  q.imageSize === 'small' ? 'max-h-36 max-w-xs' : q.imageSize === 'large' ? 'max-h-96 w-full' : 'max-h-60 max-w-md'
                }`}
              />
              <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-slate-900/70 text-white text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center space-x-1 backdrop-blur-xs">
                <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Phóng to</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Audio player DÀNH CHO TIẾNG ANH */}
        {activeExam.subjectId === 'english' && q.audioText && (
          <div className="my-3">
            <AudioPlayer speechText={q.audioText} isEnglish={true} title="Nghe đoạn âm thanh tiếng Anh" />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 1. Dạng câu hỏi có nhiều ý nhỏ (Multi-Item) */}
        {isMulti ? (
          <MultiItemQuestion
            question={q}
            userAnswers={userAnswers}
            onAnswerChange={handleSelectAnswer}
            isSubmitted={isExamFinished}
          />
        ) : (q.type === 'multiple-choice' || q.type === 'listen-choice') ? (
          /* 2. Dạng trắc nghiệm 4 lựa chọn */
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 mt-4">
            {(q.options || []).map((opt, oIdx) => {
              const isSelected = userAns === opt;
              const isCorrectAnswer = isExamFinished && opt === q.answer;
              const isWrongSelected = isExamFinished && isSelected && opt !== q.answer;

              return (
                <button
                  key={oIdx}
                  disabled={isExamFinished}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectAnswer(q.id, opt)}
                  className={`p-4 rounded-2xl font-black text-left transition border-2 flex items-center justify-between text-sm md:text-base ${
                    isCorrectAnswer
                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-950 ring-2 ring-emerald-300'
                      : isWrongSelected
                      ? 'bg-rose-100 border-rose-500 text-rose-950'
                      : isSelected
                      ? 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-400 text-rose-950 shadow-sm ring-2 ring-rose-200'
                      : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-800'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 text-xs flex items-center justify-center font-black">
                      {String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)}
                    </span>
                    <span>{opt}</span>
                  </span>
                  {isCorrectAnswer && <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />}
                  {isWrongSelected && <XCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 shrink-0" />}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ) : (q.type === 'fill-in' || q.type === 'listen-dictation') ? (
          /* 3. Dạng điền đáp án ngắn đơn */
